Abstract: The subsystem-size dependence of ground-state entanglement entropy and its crossover to thermal entropy as a function of temperature are well understood for one-dimensional (1D) gapless systems described by conformal field theory (CFT), and for free fermions with a Fermi surface in any dimension. However, little is known about the entanglement entropy for gapless fermionic systems without quasi-particles, such as a strange metal. Here we study the entanglement entropy of fermions in a solvable large-$N$ 1D lattice model akin to the Yukawa-Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (Yukawa SYK) model. In this model, two Fermi points are coupled to scalar bosons via spatially random Yukawa interactions, providing a solvable model of a strange metal when the bosons become critical at a quantum critical point. We exactly compute the second Rényi entropy of fermions in a spatial subregion in this model. Our results unravel crucial role of intra-subregion entanglement between fermionic and bosonic degrees of freedom along with the inter-subregion entanglement in understanding the ground states of such strongly coupled fermion-boson systems. We show that the crossover from thermal entropy to entanglement entropy, is captured by a single scaling ansatz, that collapses the second Rényi entropy of fermions for different subregion sizes and temperatures into a single universal curve. We further show that the universal scaling curve for the critical strange metal is well described by the standard CFT formula, albeit with an effective central charge substantially larger than the non-interacting value.

Abstract: We study Gibbs states projected onto the symmetric sector of contractible one-form symmetries. Abstract: We report muon spin relaxation/rotation ($μ$SR) measurements of the candidate three-dimensional (3D) quantum spin liquid (QSL) PbCuTe$_2$O$_6$, hosting $S=1/2$ moments, under controlled in situ [110] uniaxial compression up to $σ_{[110]}=37.7$~MPa. A small directional lattice perturbation significantly modifies the local magnetic response, while above $σ_{\rm cr}\sim10.8$\,MPa the relaxation rates are strongly enhanced and the internal-field distribution is substantially broadened. These changes occur along with the local crystalline symmetry breaking. While, no evidence for conventional static long-range magnetic order is observed, the compression drives the system towards a structurally modified and strongly correlated state in which enhanced quasi-static correlations coexist with persistent slow spin dynamics. This work demonstrates a clean and symmetry-selective route to control frustrated exchange landscape and access hidden magnetic instabilities in a 3D QSL candidate opening up the possibilities to tune other correlated systems where intrinsic coupling between magnetic and lattice degrees of freedom are relevant.

### [97] [Ghost-RISB for Correlated Electron-Phonon Systems: Application to the Hubbard-Holstein Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04816)

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种推广的ghost-rotationally-invariant-slave-boson (ghost-RISB)方法，将局域声子模式耦合到任意on-site电子自由度中，实现了在变分框架下对电子-电子和电子-声子相互作用的非微扰处理。在Hubbard-Holstein模型中与DMFT基准比较，该方法在准粒子权重和声子性质上达到定量一致，且计算成本大幅降低。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有slave-boson方法在处理电子-声子相互作用时存在局限，无法有效捕捉动力学自能效应和声子诱导的重整化效应；同时DMFT虽准确但计算成本高昂，难以快速探索强耦合与绝热声子等苛刻区域的相图。

Abstract: We develop a generalization of the ghost-rotationally-invariant slave-boson (ghost-RISB) method that incorporates local phonon modes coupled to arbitrary on-site electronic degrees of freedom, enabling a nonperturbative treatment of electron-electron and electron-phonon interactions within an efficient variational framework. The method extends the ghost-orbital construction to capture dynamical self-energy effects and phonon-induced renormalizations beyond static slave-boson approaches. Benchmarking against dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) results for the Hubbard-Holstein model, we find excellent quantitative agreement for electron quasiparticle weights and phonon properties across a wide range of coupling strengths, and it captures accurately the competition between electron-electron and electron-phonon interactions. We show that the inclusion of the ghost orbitals is crucial to accurately describe the regime of low-frequency, strongly dynamical, phonons. The extended ghost-RISB achieves this accuracy at a fraction of the computational cost of DMFT, due to its self-consistency rooted in static observables instead of dynamical ones, enabling rapid exploration of correlated electron-phonon phase diagrams. We exploit this advantage to characterize the most demanding regime of strong coupling and adiabatic phonons. Our analysis shows a suppression of the superconducting order parameter, which is interpreted as a Franck-Condon-like reduction of the overlap between the phonon wavefunctions associated with empty and doubly-occupied sites in the bipolaronic regime.

</details>


### [98] [Complex field-induced magnetic phases and anisotropic magnetotransport in off-stoichiometric CeCuBi2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04946)
*Vikas Chahar,Shivam Rathod,Sneh,Lipika,Shobha Singh,Balwant Singh Chauhan,Subhadeep Bej,Weida Yin,Yin-Chen Huang,Rie Y. Umetsu,Ratnamala Chatterjee,Kaustuv Manna*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了CeCuBi2单晶的结构、角度依赖的磁性与磁输运性质，发现其为各向异性近藤反铁磁体（TN=14 K），具有弱重费米子行为、多重场致亚磁相变、慢自旋动力学及室温下约22%的大磁阻和约10.9%的各向异性磁阻（AMR），揭示了近藤关联、磁各向异性与场可调自旋构型之间的强耦合效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强关联量子材料中近藤关联、磁各向异性与场调谐自旋构型之间的相互作用，寻找研究各向异性量子现象的理想平台。

Method: 采用结构表征、角度依赖的磁性测量（磁化率、交流磁化率、比热）、电输运测量（电阻率、磁电阻、各向异性磁阻）等手段，对CeCuBi2单晶进行系统研究。

Result: （1）CeCuBi2在TN=14 K发生反铁磁有序，具有强各向异性和弱重费米子行为；（2）电阻率在约47 K出现宽峰，表明近藤驱动的重费米子特性；（3）磁化测量揭示多重场致亚磁相，交流磁化率显示中间场致磁态中慢自旋动力学和自旋玻璃样行为；（4）观察到大的各向异性磁输运响应，300 K、9 T下磁阻约22%，蝴蝶状AMR值达约10.9%。

Conclusion: CeCuBi2表现出近藤关联、磁各向异性与场可调自旋构型的强相互作用，是探索关联和各向异性量子现象的可能平台。

Abstract: We report a detailed study on the structural, angle-dependent magnetic and magnetotransport properties of highly anisotropic off-stoichiometric CeCuBi2 single crystals. Our results reveal CeCuBi2 as an anisotropic Kondo antiferromagnet exhibiting complex field-induced magnetic behavior and unusual magnetotransport properties. Magnetic susceptibility and specific heat measurements reveal antiferromagnetic (AFM) ordering below TN = 14 K with strong anisotropy and weak heavy-fermion behavior. Electrical transport measurements show highly anisotropic resistivity and a broad hump around 47 K, indicative of Kondo-driven heavy-fermion behavior. Magnetization measurements reveal multiple field-induced metamagnetic phases, while AC susceptibility measurements indicate slow spin dynamics and spin-glass-like behavior in intermediate field-induced magnetic states. Furthermore, we observe large and strongly anisotropic magneto transport responses, including room-temperature magnetoresistance of approximately 22% at 300 K and 9 T and butterfly-like anisotropic magnetoresistance with AMR values reaching approximately 10.9%. These results highlight a strong interplay among Kondo correlations, magnetic anisotropy, and field-tunable spin configurations, making CeCuBi2 a possible platform for exploring correlated and anisotropic quantum phenomena.

</details>


### [99] [Direct Evidence for Robust Bulk Band Gap Across the Charge Density Wave Transition in TiSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05059)
*Turgut Yilmaz,Anil Rajapitamahuni,Muhammad Awais Fiaz,Zhenxian Liu,Jerzy T. Sadowski,Abdullah Al-Mahboob,Asish K. Kundu,Shawna M. Hollen,Boris Sinkovic,Elio Vescovo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过高分辨率角分辨光电子能谱直接追踪TiSe2中电荷密度波转变的温度演化，发现基础带隙大小在转变前后保持不变，表明TiSe2并非发生温度驱动的电子带隙打开，而是晶格对称性破缺重构折叠了预先存在的带绝缘体的电子结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TiSe2中电荷密度波转变机制争议数十年，核心问题在于转变是否涉及体带隙的打开或增强，需要直接实验证据来判定。

Method: 使用高分辨率角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES），在CDW转变温度TCDW=200 K附近追踪体带边随温度的演化。

Result: 发现从高温正常相到160 K，基本带隙大小保持不变；CDW仅引起带折叠特征和谱权重重新分布，底层带极值不受扰动。

Conclusion: TiSe2不发生温度驱动的电子带隙打开，其转变由晶格对称性破缺重构主导，折叠而非打开预先存在的带绝缘体的电子结构。

Abstract: The mechanism driving the charge density wave (CDW) transition in TiSe2 has been debated for decades, with proposals ranging from an excitonic insulator to a lattice-driven instability. A central question remains whether the transition involves an opening or enhancement of the bulk band gap. Using high-resolution angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, we directly track the temperature evolution of the bulk band edges across the CDW transition at TCDW = 200 K. Contrary to the expectation of a gap-opening transition, we find that the size of the fundamental band gap remains constant from the high-temperature normal phase down to 160 K. While the CDW induces clear band-folding signatures and spectral weight redistribution, the underlying band extrema are unperturbed. These results demonstrate that TiSe2 does not undergo a temperature-driven electronic gap opening. Instead, they support a scenario where the transition is governed by a lattice symmetry-breaking reconstruction that folds, but does not gap, the electronic structure of a pre-existing band insulator.

</details>


### [100] [A fractional quantum Hall factory on quantum processors: constant-depth preparation of clustered non-Abelian states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05140)
*Cheng Xu,Ching Hua Lee,Hong-Hao Tu,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文展示了在可编程量子硬件上，非阿贝尔分数量子霍尔（FQH）态（尤其是簇状的非阿贝尔态）比常见的阿贝尔Laughlin态更容易制备——前者只需常数深度（深度3）的并行量子电路，而后者需要线性深度的顺序电路。作者在IBM Heron处理器上制备了包含18个家族、覆盖全部156个量子比特的FQH态，包括154量子比特的Read-Rezayi Z4态，并通过测量验证了分数量子空穴电荷和非阿贝尔编织数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔任意子作为分数量子霍尔物质中的奇异激发，在传统平台上极难实现。作者希望通过可编程量子硬件平台来制备和研究这些更奇异的FQH激发，以突破传统平台在规模和多变性上的限制。

Method: 作者提出了一个系统性的框架来编目可能的FQH态，并设计了并行量子制备电路。核心发现是：簇状非阿贝尔FQH态（如Read-Rezayi态）的制备电路深度与系统尺寸无关（仅需常数深度，实测深度3），而阿贝尔Laughlin态需要线性深度的顺序电路链。作者在IBM Heron处理器上实现了这一框架，并辅以干涉测量扩展来验证非阿贝尔e/4准空穴的编织数据。

Result: 制备了深度为3的parafermionic Read-Rezayi Z3态（8到118量子比特），完整根采样扩展到154量子比特、104电子的Read-Rezayi Z4态；18个FQH态家族覆盖IBM Heron处理器全部156个量子比特。测量恢复预期的分数量子空穴电荷，簇状态中每个对称选择shot的电荷估计器精确，并通过干涉扩展测量了非阿贝尔e/4准空穴的编织数据。

Conclusion: 这项工作建立了在量子处理器上研究FQH物理的可扩展路线，开辟了制备和探测非阿贝尔拓扑物质的新途径，其规模和多样性远超传统平台所能达到的范围。

Abstract: Non-Abelian anyons arise as exotic excitations in fractional quantum Hall (FQH) matter and have proved very elusive to realize in conventional platforms. In this work, we show that on a programmable quantum hardware platform, the more exotic FQH excitations are the less costly ones to prepare: clustered non-Abelian FQH states admit parallel quantum preparation circuits whose two-qubit depth is independent of system size, while constructing the more common Abelian Laughlin state requires a sequential circuit chain with linear depth. The centerpiece of this work is our new systematic framework for cataloging possible FQH states and preparing them on quantum circuits at unprecedented scale and variety. Our prepared parafermionic Read--Rezayi $\mathbb{Z}_3$ state holds depth 3 from 8 to 118 qubits, and full root sampling extends to a 154-qubit, 104-electron Read--Rezayi $\mathbb{Z}_4$ state. In all, our demonstrated 18-family catalog of prepared FQH states extends to all 156 qubits of an IBM Heron processor, limited only by existing hardware scale. Measurements on the prepared states recover the expected fractional quasihole charges, with the charge estimator exact in every symmetry-selected shot for the clustered states, and braiding data of the non-Abelian $e/4$ quasihole measured via interferometric extensions. Our work establishes a scalable route to studying FQH physics on quantum processors and opens new avenues for preparing and probing non-Abelian topological matter far beyond the reach of conventional platforms.

</details>

### [101] [Rational spanning sets of level-one cusp forms from Eisenstein series at prime levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04241)
*Tianyu Ni*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文针对全模群 SL₂(ℤ) 上权为 k 的尖点形式空间 S_k，通过素数水平 Eisenstein 级数构造了一个显式的有理张成子集，并利用非中心二次扭曲 L-值确定尖点形式作为核心证明工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模形式理论中，尖点形式空间 S_k 的显式张成集构造是一个基础且重要的问题。作者希望通过 Eisenstein 级数（一般构造更简单）来张成尖点形式空间，并获得有理系数的显式基，为后续研究（如 L-值计算、算术应用）提供工具。

Method: 论文的核心方法是利用非中心二次扭曲 L-值来判定尖点形式。即证明：若一个 cusp form 的若干非中心二次扭曲 L-值均为零，则该形式为零。这一判定准则使得可以通过这些 L-值来刻画尖点形式空间，进而从素数水平的 Eisenstein 级数出发构造出有理张成子集。

Result: 论文成功构造了 S_k 的一个显式有理张成子集，该子集由素数水平的 Eisenstein 级数组合得到，为尖点形式空间提供了具体的生成元素。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Eisenstein 级数与尖点形式通过二次扭曲 L-值建立了新的联系，提供了尖点形式空间的有理显式张成集，对模形式的算术理论具有潜在的应用价值。

Abstract: Let $S_k$ be the space of cusp forms of weight $k$ for the full modular group ${\rm SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$. In this paper, we will exhibit an explicit rational spanning subset of $S_k$ constructed from Eisenstein series at prime levels. The main ingredient of our proof is to show a result on determining cusp forms by noncentral quadratic-twist $L$-values.

</details>


### [102] [The distribution and the structure of the maximum of partial sums in families of trace functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04249)
*Kilian Lebreton*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在Autissier、Bonolis和Lamzouri先前工作的基础上，对一类m-周期复值函数族（如Kloosterman和、Birch和）的部分和最大值分布，获得了更优的尾部估计，首次在较大的均匀范围内给出了分布函数对数的渐近公式。此外，还证明了该类函数族中部分和最大值的一个"结构定理"，表明该函数类中大部分范数较大的部分和接近其虚部，且最大值在m/2附近取得，这与Dirichlet特征和的最大值分布结果形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在解析数论中，理解Kloosterman和、Birch和等指数和的部分和最大值分布是一个重要问题。Autissier-Bonolis-Lamzouri已获得该类函数族部分和最大值分布函数的均匀估计，但该估计不够精确。本文旨在改进尾部估计以获得渐近公式，并深入理解最大值取到时的结构性质，与Dirichlet特征的情形形成对比。

Method: 在与前人相同的假设条件下，作者通过更精细的分析改进了尾部估计，从而在大均匀范围内得到分布函数对数的渐近公式。在结构定理的证明中，作者证明该函数类中范数较大的部分和普遍接近其虚部，且最大值在m/2处附近达到，揭示了该类周期性函数族独特的统计结构。

Result: 获得了改进的尾部估计，首次在大的均匀范围内给出了分布函数对数的渐近公式。证明了"结构定理"：在该函数类中，大多数范数较大的部分和接近其虚部，且最大值在m/2附近达到。这一行为与Bober-Goldmakher-Granville-Koukoulopoulos、Lamzouri以及Lamzouri-Nath关于Dirichlet特征和最大值分布的结果形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 本文的结果显著推进了对一类m-周期复值函数族部分和最大值分布的理解，不仅给出了更精确的渐近公式，还揭示了这类函数族（如Kloosterman和、Birch和）与Dirichlet特征之间在最大值结构上的本质区别。

Abstract: In \cite{ABL21}, Autissier, Bonolis and Lamzouri obtained uniform estimates for the distribution function of the maximum of partial sums of a class of families of $m$-periodic complex-valued functions under certain conditions. For example, these conditions are verified by Kloosterman or Birch sums.
  In this article, under the same assumptions, we obtain an improved estimate for the tail of the distribution of the maximum of partial sums in these families, which gives for the first time an asymptotic formula for the logarithm of the distribution function, in a large uniform range.
  Furthermore, we prove a "structure theorem" for the maximum of partial sums of a family of complex $m$-periodic functions in our class, which shows that universally over this class, most of the partial sums with large norm are close to their imaginary part, and the maximum is attained around $m/2$. This is in sharp constrast with the results of Bober-Golmaker-Granville-Koukoulopoulos \cite{BGGK18}, Lamzouri \cite{Lam24} and Lamzouri-Nath \cite{LN24} for the distribution of the maximum of character sums in various families of Dirichlet characters.

</details>


### [103] [The $5$-divisible integer group determinants for the elementary abelian group of order 25](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04267)
*Chatchawan Panraksa*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文确定了群 C_5×C_5 的群行列式的全体整数值，证明所有 5^8 的整数倍均可由某个群行列式取到，从而完全解决了 Taussky–Todd 整数群行列式问题对阶为 25 的所有群的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作已确定 C_5×C_5 群行列式中与 5 互素的值，并证明所有 5-可分的值都被 5^8 整除，但反向包含关系（即每个 5^8 的倍数能否被取到）尚未证明，这是完成阶为 25 的群分类的最后一块拼图。

Method: 作者使用一个一般性的平移（shift）判据，并构造三个显式多项式，其群行列式分别为 5^8、2·5^8 和 5^9。通过证明 5^8Z 包含于 S(G)，结合已有的正整数部分分类，得出完整的集合刻画。再利用 25 阶群的已知分类结果完成所有阶为 25 的群的求解。

Result: 证明了反向包含 5^8Z ⊆ S(G)，从而得到 S(G) = {m ∈ Z : m ≡ ±1 或 ±7 (mod 25)} ∪ 5^8Z 的完整刻画。结合 C_25 的已知结果，完成了阶为 25 的所有群的 Taussky–Todd 整数群行列式问题。

Conclusion: C_5×C_5 的整数群行列式集合被完全确定，附带地解决了阶为 25 的全部群上的 Taussky–Todd 问题，标志着这一问题在该阶数下的最终解决。

Abstract: Let $G=C_5\times C_5$, and let $S(G)$ denote the set of integer values of its group determinant. Previous work determines the values in $S(G)$ coprime to $5$ and proves that every $5$-divisible value is divisible by $5^8$. We prove the converse inclusion $5^8\mathbb{Z}\subseteq S(G)$. Consequently, $S(G)=\{m\in\mathbb{Z}:m\equiv\pm1\text{ or }\pm7\pmod{25}\}\cup5^8\mathbb{Z}$. The proof uses a general shift criterion and three explicit polynomials whose group determinants are $5^8$, $2\cdot5^8$, and $5^9$. Together with the known classification for $C_{25}$, this completes the Taussky--Todd integer group determinant problem for all groups of order $25$.

</details>


### [104] [On the Burgess bound and Pythagorean triples involving primitive roots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04391)
*Hai-Liang Wu,He-Xia Ni*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文结合Pierce和Xu的高维Burgess界与经典一维Burgess界，证明了对于任意足够大的素数p，存在毕达哥拉斯三元组(a_p,b_p,c_p)满足0<a_p,b_p,c_p<p，且a_pb_p/2是模p的原根，从而确认了孙智伟对所有足够大素数的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决孙智伟提出的猜想：是否存在以模p为界的毕达哥拉斯三元组，使得其面积的一半（a_pb_p/2）成为模p的原根。原根在数论中具有重要地位，这一猜想将几何对象（直角三角形）与代数结构（有限域乘法群）联系起来。

Method: 作者将Pierce和Xu关于高维情况下的Burgess界结果与经典的一维Burgess界相结合。Burgess界是数论中用于估计特征和模的经典工具，通过这种结合可以在足够大的素数范围内建立所需的原根存在性。

Result: 本文证明了对所有足够大的素数p，孙智伟猜想成立，即存在毕达哥拉斯三元组(a_p,b_p,c_p)（各分量均在0到p之间），使得a_pb_p/2是模p的原根。

Conclusion: 该结果确认了孙智伟猜想在足够大素数情境下的正确性，展示了Burgess界在不同维度下的复合应用在解决具体数论猜想中的有效性。

Abstract: By combining the recent results of Pierce and Xu on the higher-dimensional Burgess bound with the classical one-dimensional Burgess bound, we prove that for any sufficiently large prime $p$, there exists a Pythagorean triple $(a_p,b_p,c_p)$ with $a_p,b_p,c_p\in\mathbb{Z}\cap(0,p)$ such that $a_pb_p/2$ is a primitive root modulo $p$. This confirms a conjecture of Z.-W. Sun for all sufficiently large primes.

</details>


### [105] [Powers as Fibonacci Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04445)
*Benjamin Earp-Lynch,Simon Earp-Lynch,Omar Kihel,Pagdame Tiebekabe*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究方程 y^a = Σ_{i=1}^k F_{n_i}（其中 y,a≥2 均为正整数且 k≥3），该方程可转化为整数 Zeckendorf 表示问题。利用对数线性形式的下界和 Baker-Davenport 约简方法，作者完整解决了 k=3（y≤25000）、k=4（y≤1000）、k=5（y≤40）及 k=6（y≤3）情况下的方程求解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在探讨斐波那契数的和是否能表示为某个整数的完全幂，属于数论中幂数与递推序列和的交叉问题。此类方程的研究有助于深入理解斐波那契数的结构性质及 Zeckendorf 表示的唯一性，同时为相关丢番图方程的研究提供新的方法与结果。

Method: 本文将原方程转化为整数 Zeckendorf 表示问题，利用对数线性形式的下界（linear forms in logarithms）来约束可能解的规模，并结合 Baker-Davenport 约简方法（Baker-Davenport reduction）对候选解进行精细筛选，从而在指定的 y 和 k 范围内实现完全求解。

Result: 该研究完全解决了以下范围内的方程：k=3 时 y≤25000；k=4 时 y≤1000；k=5 时 y≤40；k=6 时 y≤3。所有范围内的所有正整数解均被完全确定。

Conclusion: 通过将对数线性形式界与 Baker-Davenport 约简方法结合，作者成功在较大参数范围内完整求解了涉及斐波那契数之和的幂方程，展示了该方法的有效性，并为更一般情形的研究提供了可靠框架。

Abstract: We examine the equation $y^{a}=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{k}F_{n_{i}}$ for positive integers $y,a\geq 2$ and $k\geq3$. This equation can be expressed as a problem in terms of the Zeckendorf representations of integers. Using bounds on linear forms in logarithms and Baker-Davenport reduction methods, we are able to completely solve the equation for $ y\leq 25000$ when $k=3$, for $y\leq 1000$ when $k= 4$, for $y\leq 40$ when $k= 5$, and for $y\leq 3$ when $k=6$.

</details>


### [106] [Regularized Multitangent Functions and Reduction Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04492)
*Jia Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了一种关于stuffle正则化多正切函数的直接解析理论，证明了它们可以约化为有限个单调切函数的线性组合，且不依赖mould calculus。通过渐近比较与部分分式分解，给出约化系数的显式公式，并由此推导出一族正则化多重zeta值之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多正切函数是多重Hurwitz zeta函数的周期化变体，在数论与组合学中具有重要地位。此前对stuffle正则化多正切函数约化的研究依赖于mould calculus这一较复杂的形式工具。本文旨在发展一套直接的解析理论，避免mould calculus，从而更清晰地揭示正则化多正切函数的结构与约化机制。

Method: (1) 建立一侧截断的多重Hurwitz zeta函数与其在特殊参数T_N-H(s)处的stuffle正则化之间的渐近比较；(2) 将此比较应用于对称多正切截断，构造出亚纯的、1-周期的正则化多正切函数；(3) 对每个被加项进行显式部分分式分解，并结合移动截断范围的渐近估计，推导约化系数的公式；(4) 利用Im s→±∞的极限确定常数项及单调切函数系数，其中例外指标{1}^r通过正弦商生成函数求值。

Result: 获得了stuffle正则化多正切函数约化为有限个单调切函数线性组合的显式公式，约化系数以stuffle正则化多重zeta值表出。当指标含有大于1的项时，常数项与单调切函数系数均为零；对于例外指标{1}^r，通过正弦商生成函数给出了求值。作为推论，得到了一族正则化多重zeta值之间的新关系。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种完全解析的、不依赖mould calculus的处理stuffle正则化多正切函数的理论框架。该理论不仅给出了约化的显式公式，还揭示了正则化多正切函数与多重Hurwitz zeta函数在特殊调和截断参数下的深刻联系，并导出了正则化多重zeta值的有效关系族。

Abstract: We develop a direct analytic theory of stuffle-regularized multitangent functions and prove their reduction to finite linear combinations of monotangent functions, without using mould calculus. We first establish an asymptotic comparison between one-sided truncated multiple Hurwitz zeta functions and their stuffle regularizations at the natural parameter \(T_N-H(s)\), where \(T_N\) is the harmonic truncation and \(H(s)\) is the harmonic-number function. Applying this comparison to symmetric multitangent truncations yields meromorphic, \(1\)-periodic regularized multitangent functions.
  An explicit partial-fraction decomposition of each summand, combined with asymptotic estimates for moving truncation ranges, gives formulas for the reduction coefficients in terms of stuffle-regularized multiple zeta values. The constant term and the coefficient of the monotangent \(\mathcal T(1;s)\) are determined from the limits as \(\operatorname{Im}s\to\pm\infty\): both vanish whenever the index contains an entry greater than \(1\), whereas the exceptional indices \(\{1\}^r\) are evaluated through a sine-quotient generating function. As a consequence, we obtain a family of relations among regularized multiple zeta values.

</details>


### [107] [Construction Of Non-Odd Galois Representations With Large Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04577)
*Donghyeok Lim,Christian Maire*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了具有大像但非GL_r-奇（即无穷远处不正则）的伽罗瓦表示。具体地，对每个素数p≥3、整数r≥2及满足特定条件的整数a，构造了连续伽罗瓦表示ρ: G_Q → GL_r(Q_p)，其像与GL_r(Z_p)可通约，且|tr(ρ(c))| = a（c为复共轭）。还获得了正交群O_r(Q_p)的类似结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有大像的伽罗瓦表示是算术几何中的核心问题之一。通常构造的大像伽罗瓦表示满足GL_r-奇（或无穷远处正则）这一条件，但满足该条件的表示类别有限。本文旨在突破这一限制，构造"非GL_r-奇"的大像伽罗瓦表示，从而扩展对伽罗瓦表示结构及其像的理解，特别是研究当复共轭的迹取不同值时的可能性。

Method: 对于每个素数p≥3、整数r≥2以及满足条件a ≡ r (mod 2)且a ∈ [p(1-p^r)/2, r-2]的整数a，构造连续伽罗瓦表示ρ: G_Q → GL_r(Q_p)，使其像与GL_r(Z_p)可通约，并且复共轭的迹的绝对值满足|tr(ρ(c))| = a。作者使用类似的方法也构造了正交群O_r(Q_p)中的对应表示。

Result: 成功为所有满足条件的参数(p, r, a)构造了非GL_r-奇的大像伽罗瓦表示，其像与GL_r(Z_p)可通约且满足给定的迹条件；同时为正交群O_r(Q_p)获得了平行的构造结果。

Conclusion: 本文突破了大像伽罗瓦表示必须为GL_r-奇的限制，为更大一类（非正则无穷远型）的大像伽罗瓦表示的构造提供了方法，拓展了该领域的构造技术和可实现的表示类。

Abstract: In this work, we construct Galois representations with large image that are not GL r -odd (or, equivalently, not regular at infinity). More precisely, for every prime p {\v e} 3, every integer r {\v e} 2, and every integer a P rp1 'p'1q r q{2, r '2s satisfying a '' r pmod 2q, we construct a continuous Galois representation $ρ$\,: G Q __ GL r pQ p q whose image is commensurable with GL r pZ p q and satisfies |trp$ρ$pcqq| '' a, where c denotes a complex conjugation. We also obtain analogous results for the orthogonal group O r pQ p q.

</details>


### [108] [On the $L^2$ restriction norm of large level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04597)
*Chengliang Guo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了平方自由大水平下全纯Hecke新形式在垂直测地线段上的质量等分布定理，利用水平方向的QUE有效证明，并进一步研究了整条测地线上的L²质量与扭曲L函数二阶矩的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究全纯Hecke新形式在大平方自由水平下沿垂直测地线段的mass等分布问题，这是量子唯一遍历性（QUE）在算术背景下在测地线（而非曲面整体）上的精细化研究，同时建立与L函数二阶矩之间的关联。

Method: 利用水平aspect下QUE的有效证明，将其限制在垂直测地线段上以建立mass等分布定理；对于整条测地线情形，将L²质量与扭曲L函数的二阶矩联系起来进行分析。

Result: 成功证明了垂直测地线段上的mass等分布定理，并在整条测地线情形下将问题转化为扭曲L函数二阶矩的研究。

Conclusion: 该工作将QUE理论从曲面整体推广到测地线子集上，通过L函数二阶矩建立了新的联系，为算术量子混沌领域的进一步研究提供了新工具和视角。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the mass equidistribution theorem restricted to vertical geodesic segments for holomorphic Hecke newforms of large square-free level. We utilize the effective proof of QUE in the level aspect. Moreover, we study this $L^2$ mass in the full geodesics and relate it to second moments of twisted $L$-functions.

</details>


### [109] [A proof of the Freiman-Lev conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04760)
*Yujie Wang,Min Tang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文完整证明了Freiman-Lev猜想，解决了其最后也是最困难的情况，即a_{k-2}≥2k-4且a_{k-1}≥2k-2的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Freiman-Lev猜想是关于受限和集2^∧A的一个著名猜想，此前在所有a_{k-2}≥2k-4和a_{k-1}≥2k-2的情况下仍然是开放的，本文旨在填补这一空白，完成该猜想的完整证明。

Method: 本文针对集合A={a_0,...,a_{k-1}}（其中k>7，0=a_0<...<a_{k-1}，且gcd(A)=1），通过构造和分析受限和集2^∧A={a+b: a,b∈A, a≠b}的结构，专门攻克此前未能解决的最终情况（a_{k-2}≥2k-4且a_{k-1}≥2k-2）。

Result: 成功解决了Freiman-Lev猜想的最后一个开放情形，从而完成了该猜想的完整证明。

Conclusion: Freiman-Lev猜想现已完全证明，此前所有未解决的情况均已被本文覆盖，该领域的一个重要开放问题得到了彻底解决。

Abstract: Let $A=\{a_{0}, a_{1}, \ldots, a_{k-1}\}$ be a set of $k>7$ integers such that $0=a_{0}<a_1<\cdots<a_{k-1}$ and $\gcd(A)=1$.
  The set $2^{\wedge}A=\{a+b: a, b\in A, a\neq b\}$ is called the restricted sumsets of $A$.
  Freiman-Lev conjecture is a well-known conjecture which related to restricted sumsets [V.F. Lev, Restricted set addition in groups, I. The classical setting, J. London Math. Soc. 62(2000), 27-40].
  Up to now, Freiman-Lev conjecture is still open for all $a_{k-2}\geqslant 2k-4$ and $a_{k-1}\geqslant 2k-2$.
  In this paper, we complete the proof of the Freiman-Lev conjecture by resolving this final and most challenging case.

</details>


### [110] [Adelic framed form class groups and explicit class field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04873)
*Ja Kyung Koo,Dong Hwa Shin,Dong Sung Yoon*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入“adelic framed forms”的概念，在负判别式D的原始正定二元二次型集合与SL₂(ℤ̂)的乘积上定义带框架的形式集合Q̂(D)，并研究其在SL₂(ℤ)作用下的轨道空间Ĉ(D)。作者在该空间上定义了Gauss-Dirichlet合成律的adelic类比，赋予其合适的拓扑结构后，证明Ĉ(D)与某个abelian扩张的Galois群（带Krull拓扑）拓扑同构，并将该框架与经典的Gauss合成、有限级形式类群及志村互反律统一起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Gauss合成律、形式类群和志村互反律（Shimura reciprocity）虽然在数论中分别有重要应用，但长期以来缺乏一个统一的框架将它们联系在同一体系中。本文旨在通过adelic（adele型）构造，建立一个统一的数学框架，将这些经典结果整合在一起，并为相应的Galois群提供显式的拓扑同构描述。

Method: 作者定义adelic framed forms集合Q̂(D)（包含形式Q与SL₂(ℤ̂)元素γ且满足某个单位条件的对），并通过SL₂(ℤ)的自然作用构造轨道空间Ĉ(D)。在Ĉ(D)上定义Gauss-Dirichlet合成律的显式adelic类比，同时利用离散拓扑Q(D)与profinite拓扑SL₂(ℤ̂)的乘积诱导出Ĉ(D)的商拓扑。随后证明Ĉ(D)与Gal(K^ab(𝔱^{1/∞})/K(𝔱))的拓扑群同构，其中右端Galois群赋Krull拓扑。

Result: 建立了Ĉ(D) ≃ Gal(K^ab(𝔱^{1/∞})/K(𝔱))的拓扑群同构；进一步识别出一个显式子群与Gal(K^ab/K)同构，并描述了对应的模函数特殊值上的Galois作用。这一结果将经典Gauss合成、有限级形式类群和志村互反律统一在单一adelic框架中。

Conclusion: 本文通过adelic framed forms的构造，成功地将Gauss-Dirichlet合成律（在adelic层面推广）、有限级形式类群和志村互反律凝聚为一个统一的数学理论。该工作为理解二元二次型的组合结构与其对应的算术Galois群之间的深层联系提供了新的统一视角。

Abstract: Let $D$ be a negative discriminant, and let $K=\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{D})$. Let $\mathcal{Q}(D)$ denote the set of primitive positive definite binary quadratic forms over $\mathbb{Z}$ of discriminant $D$. We introduce the set of adelic framed forms \begin{equation*} \widehat{\mathcal{Q}}(D)= \left\{(Q,\,γ)\in \mathcal{Q}(D)\times\mathrm{SL}_2(\widehat{\mathbb{Z}})~|~ Q\left(γ\begin{bmatrix}1\\0\end{bmatrix}\right)\in \widehat{\mathbb{Z}}^\times\right\} \end{equation*} and its orbit space $\widehat{C}(D)$ under the natural action of $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$. We define an explicit adelic analogue of the Gauss-Dirichlet composition law on $\widehat{C}(D)$ and endow $\widehat{C}(D)$ with the quotient topology induced by the subspace topology on $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}(D)$ inherited from the product topology on $\mathcal{Q}(D)\times\mathrm{SL}_2(\widehat{\mathbb{Z}})$, where $\mathcal{Q}(D)$ is discrete and $\mathrm{SL}_2(\widehat{\mathbb{Z}})$ has its profinite topology. We then prove that there is an isomorphism of topological groups \begin{equation*} \widehat{C}(D)\simeq\mathrm{Gal}\left(K^\mathrm{ab}(\mathfrak{t}^{1/\infty})/K(\mathfrak{t})\right), \end{equation*} where the Galois group is endowed with the Krull topology, $\mathfrak{t}$ is a positive transcendental real number, and $\mathfrak{t}^{1/\infty}=\{\sqrt[N]{\mathfrak{t}}~|~N\geq1\}$. Moreover, we identify an explicitly defined subgroup of $\widehat{C}(D)$ with $\mathrm{Gal}(K^\mathrm{ab}/K)$ and describe the corresponding Galois action on special values of modular functions. In this way, classical Gauss composition, finite-level form class groups, and Shimura reciprocity are brought together within a single adelic framework.

</details>


### [111] [Quantitative Khintchine on the parabola with non-monotonic approximation functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04878)
*Maiken Gravgaard,Simon Kristensen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Khintchine定理在度量丢番图逼近中收敛情形的定量版本，但将逼近点限制在抛物线上。与以往文献不同，文中不再要求逼近函数具有单调性，为此需要在经典数论结果（特别是Burgess短区间特征和界）中获得显式常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Khintchine定理是度量丢番图逼近领域的经典结果，但此前关于其在子流形（如抛物线）上的推广通常要求逼近函数是单调的。去除这一限制是自然且重要的推广方向，有助于更深入地理解流形上的度量丢番图逼近结构。

Method: 论文通过获得经典数论结果中的显式常数来实现这一突破，关键工具是Burgess关于短区间特征和的界的显式版本，以此取代对逼近函数单调性的依赖。

Result: 成功证明了Khintchine收敛情形在抛物线上不带单调性假设的定量版本，这比已有文献中需要单调性假设的结果更为一般。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了Khintchine定理到抛物线的收敛情形，并首次移除了逼近函数的单调性要求，为子流形上的度量丢番图逼近研究提供了新的定量工具。

Abstract: We prove a quantitative version of the convergence case of Khintchine's celebrated theorem in metric Diophantine approximation, but where the approximated points are restricted to lying on the parabola. A novel feature of our result is that unlike other results in literature, the approximating function is no longer required to be monotonic. This requires us to obtain explicit constants in classical number theoretic results, most notably in Burgess' bound for character sums in short intervals.

</details>


### [112] [Chebotarev geodesic theorem: non-split case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04923)
*Alberto Acosta Reche*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究不定四元数序的同余子群的素测地线定理，证明了其测地线版本Chebotarev密度定理以25/36+ε的指数成立，并由此推出任意不定四元数序的任意同余子群上素测地线定理以相同指数成立。证明思路是将问题归约到作者先前处理过的分裂情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 素测地线定理是数论与动力系统中的经典课题，推广到四元数代数上的同余子群需要更精细的解析数论工具。本文旨在将作者此前在分裂情形下获得的25/36+ε指数推广到一般的不定四元数序的同余子群，从而统一并改进该方向上的已有结果。

Method: 证明的核心策略是将一般不定四元数序的同余子群问题约化到分裂（split）情形。通过构造合适的对应关系，将四元数代数上的测地线计数转化为已解决的分裂情形成果，从而借助已知的指数结果完成证明。

Result: 证明了对不定四元数序的任意同余子群，测地线版本的Chebotarev密度定理以指数25/36+ε成立，并直接推出素测地线定理在相应情形下的最优指数25/36+ε。

Conclusion: 本文成功将素测地线定理的25/36+ε指数从分裂情形推广到一般不定四元数序的同余子群，填补了该方向上的空白，为相关领域提供了更一般的解析结果。

Abstract: We study the prime geodesic theorem for congruence subgroups of indefinite quaternion orders. We prove that the geodesic analogue of the Chebotarev density theorem for these groups holds with exponent $25/36 + \varepsilon$. In particular, we deduce that the prime geodesic theorem holds with exponent $25/36 + \varepsilon$ for any congruence subgroup of any indefinite quaternion order over $\mathbb{Q}$. The idea of the proof is to reduce the problem to the split case, which has been handled previously by the author.

</details>


### [113] [An Integral Mean Value Theorem for Weyl Sums over Broken Arcs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04948)
*YaoJie Guo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究短区间上指数和S(α)积分均值在特殊minor arcs子集上的渐近估计，通过结合Diophantine逼近、Vinogradov主值定理及精细的Weyl差分论证，突破了传统方法中幂次奇偶性的限制，得到了优于经典结果的上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指数和是解析数论中的核心对象，其积分均值估计在Waring问题、圆法等领域有重要应用。经典方法通常受限于幂次k的奇偶性条件（如k>log m且log k/log m<1/2的约束），作者希望突破这一限制，建立对短区间上指数和在破碎弧（broken arc）上积分均值的更强估计，从而扩展方法的应用范围。

Method: 作者的方法分为三步：(1)利用Diophantine逼近与Vinogradov主值定理建立对几乎所有α的强估计；(2)构造精细化Weyl差分论证，将差分步长划分为大范围与小范围分别处理，显著优于经典差分方法；(3)借助概率方法计算每个和项的重数（multiplicity），综合以上结果完成证明。该方法不受幂次k奇偶性的限制。

Result: 当k>log m且log k/log m<1/2时，对长度2N^θ(θ<1)的短区间上的指数和，在测度至少c>0的minor arcs子集𝔪^*上建立了积分均值估计：∫_{𝔪^*}|∑_{N_1<u<N_2}e(zu^k)|^m dz ≪_c N^{θm((2k+1)/(2k+2)+o(1))}。

Conclusion: 该估计突破了传统Weyl差分方法的偶奇性限制，为短区间指数和均值问题提供了新的证明框架，在解析数论的Waring问题等方向具有潜在应用价值。结论表明，通过精细化的差分分类和概率方法的重数计算，可以在更广泛的参数范围内获得紧致的上界估计。

Abstract: In this article, we research the mean value of integral of exponential sum $S(α)=\sum_{u\in I}e(αu^k)$, where $I$ is a short interval whose length is $2N^θ,θ<1$, and the broken arc $\mathfrak{m^*}$ is a subset of a following minor arcs \[ \mathfrak{m}=\bigcap_{j\leq k-1}\left\{α:\forall q<(\log N)^A,h<q,(h,q)=1,\Big|α-\frac{h}{q}\Big|>\frac{1}{qN^{(k-j-1/2)θ}}\right\} \] which has measure at least $c>0$. By setting $m$ is a sufficiently large number, $N$ is be sufficiently large in terms of $m$. When $k>\log m$ and $\frac{\log k}{\log m}<1/2$ we set the following estimate: \[ \int_{\mathfrak{m^*}}\bigg|\sum\limits_{{N_1}<u<{N_2}}{e(zu^k)}\bigg|^{m}\mathrm{d}z\ll_cN^{θm(\frac{2k+1}{2k+2}+o(1))} \] We can find this estimate moving beyond the even-odd restriction of powers. To get this bound, we first set a strong estimate for almost $α$ by Diophantine approximation and Vinogradov's main value theorem. Then, combining this result, we construct a refined Weyl differencing argument by partitioning the differences step into large and small range, which significantly outperforms the classical one. By the version of probability, we can calculate the multiplicity of each sum. Put them together and we can complete the proof.

</details>


### [114] [Weyl Subconvexity for $\mathrm{GL}_2$ with Simple Supercuspidal Ramification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04982)
*Liyang Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在GL2/F上建立了Weyl型次凸性界，针对在素理想q处具有简单超尖（超cuspidal）分岔的尖点自守表示，证明了四次矩估计，并由此推出Weyl型界，特别适用于奇数导子指数的非自对偶族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GL2/F自守形式的L函数研究中，次凸性界是核心问题之一。传统方法（如三次矩方法）在处理奇数导子指数的非自对偶族时失效，因此需要开发新的技术来建立Weyl型次凸性界。

Method: 对于由简单超尖局部分量定义的表示族F_t^ζ(q^3;ω)（导子为q^3，中心特征为ω），作者证明了四次矩估计。该估计对映有限位置的导子与无穷位置的分析权重进行了精细的求和分析，利用简单超尖分岔的特殊算术结构。

Result: 建立了四次矩估计：∑|L(1/2,π)|^4 ≪ C_∞^{1+ε} N_F(q)^{2+ε}，并由此推导出Weyl型界L(1/2,π) ≪ C_∞(π)^{1/4+ε} C_fin(π)^{1/6+ε}。该界适用于三次矩方法无法处理的严格非自对偶奇数导子指数族。

Conclusion: 作者在数域上的GL2中成功建立了Weyl型次凸性界，扩展了该方法的应用范围到非自对偶的奇数指数导子情形，为后续深入研究GL2及更一般群的自守L函数次凸性问题提供了重要工具。

Abstract: We establish Weyl-type subconvexity bounds in the level aspect for cuspidal automorphic representations of $\mathrm{GL}_2/F$ with simple supercuspidal ramification at a prime ideal $\mathfrak{q}$. More precisely, for the family $\mathcal{F}_t^ζ(\mathfrak{q}^3;ω)$ consisting of representations of conductor $\mathfrak{q}^3$, central character $ω$, and prescribed simple supercuspidal local component, we prove the fourth moment estimate \begin{align*} \sum_{\substack{π\in \mathcal{F}_{t}^ζ(\mathfrak{q}^3;ω) \\ C_v(π) \leq \mathbf{C}_v,\ v \mid \infty}} |L(1/2,π)|^4 \ll_{F,\varepsilon} \mathbf C_\infty^{1+\varepsilon} N_F(\mathfrak{q})^{2+\varepsilon}. \end{align*} As a consequence, we deduce the Weyl-type bound \begin{align*} L(1/2,π) \ll_{F,\varepsilon} C_{\infty}(π)^{1/4+\varepsilon} C_{\mathrm{fin}}(π)^{1/6+\varepsilon}. \end{align*} In particular, this bound applies to a genuinely non-self-dual family of odd conductor exponent, beyond the reach of the cubic moment method.

</details>
